181: // function pickSpacedStops<T extends { distance_from_start_km: number }>(
182: //   sortedStops: T[],
183: //   minSpacingKm: number,
184: //   maxCount: number
185: // ): T[] {
186: //   const picked: T[] = []
187: 
188: //   for (const stop of sortedStops) {
189: //     if (picked.length >= maxCount) break
190: 
191: //     const isFarEnough = picked.every(
192: //       (existing) =>
193: //         Math.abs(existing.distance_from_start_km - stop.distance_from_start_km) >= minSpacingKm
194: //     )
195: 
196: //     if (isFarEnough) picked.push(stop)
197: //   }
198: 
199: //   // Fallback: if strict spacing rejects too many, fill remaining slots by order.
200: //   if (picked.length < Math.min(maxCount, sortedStops.length)) {
201: //     for (const stop of sortedStops) {
202: //       if (picked.length >= maxCount) break
203: //       if (!picked.some((p) => p.distance_from_start_km === stop.distance_from_start_km)) {
204: //         picked.push(stop)
205: //       }
206: //     }
207: //   }
208: 
209: //   return picked
210: // }

220: //     return `${normalizeName(locationName)}|${latNum.toFixed(5)}|${lngNum.toFixed(5)}`
221: //   }
222: //   return `${normalizeName(locationName)}|${latitude}|${longitude}`

225: // // Generate stops from database with filtering
226: // async function generateStop(
227: //   startLat: number,
228: //   startLng: number,
229: //   destLat: number,
230: //   destLng: number
231: // ): Promise<GenerateStopResult> {
232: //   if (!supabaseAdmin) {
233: //     return {
234: //       success: false,
235: //       stops: [],
236: //       error: "Database not configured",
237: //     }
238: //   }
239: 
240: //   try {
241: //     // Calculate bounding box: ±1 degree from start/dest
242: //     const minLat = Math.min(startLat, destLat) - 1
243: //     const maxLat = Math.max(startLat, destLat) + 1
244: //     const minLng = Math.min(startLng, destLng) - 1
245: //     const maxLng = Math.max(startLng, destLng) + 1
246: 
247: //     // Query stops table with bounding box
248: //     const { data: allStops, error: queryError } = await supabaseAdmin
249: //       .from("stops")
250: //       .select("*")
251: //       .gte("latitude", minLat)
252: //       .lte("latitude", maxLat)
253: //       .gte("longitude", minLng)
254: //       .lte("longitude", maxLng)
255: 
256: //     if (queryError) {
257: //       return {
258: //         success: false,
259: //         stops: [],
260: //         error: queryError.message,
261: //       }
262: //     }
263: 
264: //     if (!allStops || allStops.length === 0) {
265: //       console.log("[2/4] generateStop diagnostics", {
266: //         totalCandidates: 0,
267: //         afterProximity: 0,
268: //         afterCorridor: 0,
269: //       })
270: 
271: //       return {
272: //         success: true,
273: //         stops: [],
274: //         totalCandidates: 0,
275: //         afterProximity: 0,
276: //         afterCorridor: 0,
277: //       }
278: //     }
279: 
280: //     // Calculate direct distance between start and destination
281: //     const directDistance = calculateDistance(startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng)
282: 
283: //     // Calculate the true lateral (perpendicular) distance from a stop to the A→B line.
284: //     // This is far more reliable than min(distFromStart, distFromDest) or sumDist ellipses,
285: //     // which either miss on-route stops or allow far-off-route ones through.
286: //     // Returns { lateralKm, tRaw } for a point relative to the A→B route line.
287: //     // tRaw < 0 means the stop is "behind" the start; tRaw > 1 means past the destination.
288: //     const routeProjection = (lat: number, lng: number): { lateralKm: number; tRaw: number } => {
289: //       if (directDistance <= 0) {
290: //         return { lateralKm: calculateDistance(startLat, startLng, lat, lng), tRaw: 0 }
291: //       }
292: //       const avgLatRad = ((startLat + destLat) / 2) * Math.PI / 180
293: //       const scaleX = Math.cos(avgLatRad)
294: //       const vx = (destLng - startLng) * scaleX
295: //       const vy = destLat - startLat
296: //       const wx = (lng - startLng) * scaleX
297: //       const wy = lat - startLat
298: //       const vLenSq = vx * vx + vy * vy
299: //       const tRaw = vLenSq > 1e-12 ? (wx * vx + wy * vy) / vLenSq : 0
300: //       const tClamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, tRaw))
301: //       const projLat = startLat + tClamped * (destLat - startLat)
302: //       const projLng = startLng + tClamped * (destLng - startLng)
303: //       return { lateralKm: calculateDistance(lat, lng, projLat, projLng), tRaw }
304: //     }
305: 
306: //     // Filter and enrich stops with distance calculations
307: //     const enrichedStops = allStops
308: //       .map((stop) => {
309: //         const distFromStart = calculateDistance(startLat, startLng, stop.latitude, stop.longitude)
310: //         const distFromDest = calculateDistance(stop.latitude, stop.longitude, destLat, destLng)
311: //         const { lateralKm, tRaw } = routeProjection(stop.latitude, stop.longitude)
312: //         // Reject stops behind the start or past the destination (5% tolerance)
313: //         const isForwardOnRoute = tRaw >= -0.05 && tRaw <= 1.05
314: 
315: //         return {
316: //           ...stop,
317: //           distance_from_start_km: Math.round(distFromStart * 10) / 10,
318: //           distance_to_dest_km: Math.round(distFromDest * 10) / 10,
319: //           distance_to_route_km: Math.round(lateralKm * 10) / 10,
320: //           is_between_start_and_dest: lateralKm <= 150 && isForwardOnRoute,
321: //         }
322: //       })
323: 
324: //     // Filter: lateral proximity ≤150 km AND forward on route (not behind start or past dest).
325: //     const afterProximity = enrichedStops.filter((stop) => stop.is_between_start_and_dest)
326: //     const rejectedByProximity = enrichedStops.filter((stop) => !stop.is_between_start_and_dest)
327: 
328: //     // No separate corridor filter needed — lateral distance already handles it.
329: //     const afterCorridor = afterProximity
330: //     const rejectedByCorridor: typeof afterProximity = []
331: 
332: //     const orderedStops = afterCorridor
333: //       // Sort by distance from start
334: //       .sort((a, b) => a.distance_from_start_km - b.distance_from_start_km)
335: 
336: //     const targetPlannedStops = Math.max(2, Math.min(8, Math.round(directDistance / 220)))
337: //     const minSpacingKm = Math.max(60, Math.round(directDistance / (targetPlannedStops + 1) * 0.5))
338: //     const filteredStops = pickSpacedStops(orderedStops, minSpacingKm, targetPlannedStops)
339: 
340: //     console.log("[2/4] generateStop diagnostics", {
341: //       totalCandidates: allStops.length,
342: //       rejectedByProximity: rejectedByProximity.length,
343: //       afterProximity: afterProximity.length,
344: //       rejectedByCorridor: rejectedByCorridor.length,
345: //       afterCorridor: afterCorridor.length,
346: //       plannedStops: filteredStops.length,
347: //       targetPlannedStops,
348: //       minSpacingKm,
349: //     })
350: 
351: //     if (rejectedByProximity.length > 0) {
352: //       console.log("[2/4] rejected by proximity (>100km)", {
353: //         count: rejectedByProximity.length,
354: //         sample: rejectedByProximity.slice(0, 3).map((s) => ({
355: //           id: s.id,
356: //           name: s.location_name,
357: //           distance_to_route_km: s.distance_to_route_km,
358: //         })),
359: //       })
360: //     }
361: 
362: //     if (rejectedByCorridor.length > 0) {
363: //       console.log("[2/4] rejected by corridor", {
364: //         count: rejectedByCorridor.length,
365: //         sample: rejectedByCorridor.slice(0, 3).map((s) => ({
366: //           id: s.id,
367: //           name: s.location_name,
368: //           distance_from_start_km: s.distance_from_start_km,
369: //           distance_to_dest_km: s.distance_to_dest_km,
370: //         })),
371: //       })
372: //     }
373: 
374: //     return {
375: //       success: true,
376: //       stops: filteredStops,
377: //       totalCandidates: allStops.length,
378: //       afterProximity: afterProximity.length,
379: //       afterCorridor: afterCorridor.length,
380: //     }
381: //   } catch (error) {
382: //     return {

384: //       stops: [],
385: //       error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error",
386: //     }
387: //   }

431:

638: //       // Pass 3: Tight spacing (25% initial), minimal DB overlap (15km)

640: //         const pass3Spacing = Math.max(5, Math.ceil(initialSpacingKm * 0.25))

642: //       }
643: 
644: //       // Pass 4: Final fallback with very loose spacing (3km)
645: //       if (plannedCustomCandidates.length < targetCustomStops) {
646: //         addMoreCandidates(customStopCandidates, 3)
647: //       }
648: 
649: //       if (plannedCustomCandidates.length >= targetCustomStops) {
650: //         console.log(`[3/4] Adaptive fallback filled to ${plannedCustomCandidates.length} stops (target: ${targetCustomStops})`)
651: //       } else {
652: //         console.log(`[3/4] Adaptive fallback partially filled: ${plannedCustomCandidates.length} of ${targetCustomStops} (initial spacing: ${initialSpacingKm}km)`)
653: //       }
654: //     }
655: //     // Sort candidates by distance from start so we assign day indices sequentially
656: //     // along the route rather than by raw progress fraction (which clusters at extremes).
657: //     const sortedForDayAssign = [...plannedCustomCandidates].sort(
658: //       (a, b) => a.distance_from_start_km - b.distance_from_start_km
659: //     )
660: 
661: //     // Divide the full route into equal-km buckets — one per trip day.
662: //     // Each stop is assigned to the bucket its distance falls into.
663: //     // If a day bucket ends up empty, the nearest stop from an adjacent bucket
664: //     // is borrowed to fill it, preventing phantom empty days.
665: //     const totalKmForDays = directDistanceKm > 0 ? directDistanceKm : 1
666: //     const kmPerDay = totalKmForDays / safeTripDays
667: 
668: //     // First pass: assign by km bucket
669: //     const dayBuckets: number[] = sortedForDayAssign.map((candidate) => {
670: //       const distKm = Number(candidate.distance_from_start_km)
671: //       return Math.min(safeTripDays - 1, Math.floor(distKm / kmPerDay))
672: //     })
673: 
674: //     // Second pass: identify empty days and fill from adjacent stops
675: //     const bucketCounts = Array.from({ length: safeTripDays }, () => 0)
676: //     dayBuckets.forEach((d) => { bucketCounts[d] = (bucketCounts[d] || 0) + 1 })
677: 
678: //     const filledBuckets = [...dayBuckets]
679: //     for (let day = 0; day < safeTripDays; day++) {
680: //       if (bucketCounts[day] > 0) continue
681: //       // Find the nearest stop by km distance to the center of this empty day
682: //       const dayCenterKm = (day + 0.5) * kmPerDay
683: //       let bestIdx = -1
684: //       let bestDelta = Infinity
685: //       sortedForDayAssign.forEach((candidate, idx) => {
686: //         const delta = Math.abs(Number(candidate.distance_from_start_km) - dayCenterKm)
687: //         if (delta < bestDelta) { bestDelta = delta; bestIdx = idx }
688: //       })
689: //       if (bestIdx >= 0) {
690: //         filledBuckets[bestIdx] = day
691: //         bucketCounts[day] = 1
692: //       }
693: //     }
694: 
695: //     const customStopsToInsert = sortedForDayAssign.map((candidate, idx) => ({
696: //       trip_id: candidate.trip_id,
697: //       location_name: candidate.location_name,
698: //       latitude: candidate.latitude,
699: //       longitude: candidate.longitude,
700: //       place_type: candidate.place_type,
701: //       day_index: filledBuckets[idx],
702: //     }))
703: 
704: //     // Deduplicate generated custom stops before touching DB.
705: //     const uniqueCustomStops = [] as typeof customStopsToInsert
706: //     const seenKeys = new Set<string>()
707: //     for (const stop of customStopsToInsert) {
708: //       const key = buildCustomStopKey(stop.location_name as string, stop.latitude as string, stop.longitude as string)
709: //       if (seenKeys.has(key)) continue
710: //       seenKeys.add(key)
711: //       uniqueCustomStops.push(stop)
712: //     }
713: 
714: //     let insertedCustomStopsCount = 0
715: 
716: //     // Save planned custom stops to custom_stops table
717: //     if (uniqueCustomStops.length > 0) {
718: //       const { data: existingCustomStops, error: existingError } = await supabaseAdmin
719: //         .from("custom_stops")
720: //         .select("location_name, latitude, longitude")
721: //         .eq("trip_id", tripId)
722: 
723: //       if (existingError) {
724: //         return {
725: //           success: false,
726: //           stopsGenerated: 0,
727: //           totalFetched,
728: //           afterDedup: customStopCandidates.length,
729: //           error: existingError.message,
730: //         }
731: //       }
732: 
733: //       const existingKeys = new Set(
734: //         (existingCustomStops || []).map((stop) =>
735: //           buildCustomStopKey(stop.location_name, stop.latitude, stop.longitude)
736: //         )
737: //       )
738: 
739: //       const finalCustomStopsToInsert = uniqueCustomStops.filter((stop) => {
740: //         const key = buildCustomStopKey(stop.location_name as string, stop.latitude as string, stop.longitude as string)
741: //         return !existingKeys.has(key)
742: //       })
743: 
744: //       if (finalCustomStopsToInsert.length > 0) {
745: //         const { error: insertError } = await supabaseAdmin
746: //           .from("custom_stops")
747: //           .insert(finalCustomStopsToInsert)
748: 
749: //         if (insertError) {
750: //           console.log("[3/4] generateCustomStop diagnostics", {
751: //             totalFetched,
752: //             afterDedup: customStopCandidates.length,
753: //             planned: finalCustomStopsToInsert.length,
754: //             inserted: 0,
755: //           })
756: 
757: //           return {
758: //             success: false,
759: //             stopsGenerated: 0,
760: //             totalFetched,
761: //             afterDedup: customStopCandidates.length,
762: //             error: insertError.message,
763: //           }
764: //         }
765: 
766: //         insertedCustomStopsCount = finalCustomStopsToInsert.length
767: //       }
768: 
769: //       console.log("[3/4] custom stop dedupe", {
770: //         generated: customStopsToInsert.length,
771: //         uniqueGenerated: uniqueCustomStops.length,
772: //         existingInDb: (existingCustomStops || []).length,
773: //         insertedNew: insertedCustomStopsCount,
774: //         targetCustomStops,
775: //         tripDurationDays: safeTripDays,
776: //       })
777: //     }
778: 
779: //     console.log("[3/4] generateCustomStop diagnostics", {
780: //       totalFetched,
781: //       afterDedup: customStopCandidates.length,
782: //       planned: uniqueCustomStops.length,
783: //       inserted: insertedCustomStopsCount,
784: //       targetCustomStops,
785: //       directDistanceKm: Math.round(directDistanceKm),
786: //     })

788: //     return {
789: //       success: true,
790: //       stopsGenerated: insertedCustomStopsCount,
791: //       totalFetched,

990: // async function processTripGenerationQueue(): Promise<void> {
991: //   if (isTripQueueProcessing) return
992: //   isTripQueueProcessing = true
993: 
994: //   while (tripGenerationQueue.length > 0) {
995: //     const nextJob = tripGenerationQueue.shift()
996: //     if (!nextJob) continue
997: 
998: //     try {
999: //       await processTripGenerationJob(nextJob)
1000: //     } catch (error) {
1001: //       console.error("[queue] ❌ Trip generation job failed", {
1002: //         tripId: nextJob.tripId,
1003: //         error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
1004: //       })
1005: 
1006: //       if (supabaseAdmin) {
1007: //         await supabaseAdmin
1008: //           .from("trips")
1009: //           .update({ status: "planned" })
1010: //           .eq("id", nextJob.tripId)
1011: //       }
1012: //     }
1013: //   }
1014: 
1015: //   isTripQueueProcessing = false
1016: // }

8: // Maximum perpendicular distance a stop can be from the actual route polyline (km)
9: const ROUTE_PROXIMITY_THRESHOLD_KM = 30
10: 
11: function calculateDistance(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number): number {
12:   const R = 6371 // km
13:   const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180
14:   const dLng = (lng2 - lng1) * Math.PI / 180
15:   const a =
16:     Math.sin(dLat/2) * Math.sin(dLat/2) +
17:     Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) *
18:     Math.sin(dLng/2) * Math.sin(dLng/2)
19:   const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1-a))
20:   return R * c
21: }
22: 
23: function decodePolyline(encoded: string): Array<{ lat: number; lng: number }> {
24:   const poly: Array<{ lat: number; lng: number }> = []
25:   let index = 0, lat = 0, lng = 0
26:   while (index < encoded.length) {
27:     let b, shift = 0, result = 0
28:     do { b = encoded.charCodeAt(index++) - 63; result |= (b & 0x1f) << shift; shift += 5 } while (b >= 0x20)
29:     lat += (result & 1) !== 0 ? ~(result >> 1) : result >> 1
30:     shift = 0; result = 0
31:     do { b = encoded.charCodeAt(index++) - 63; result |= (b & 0x1f) << shift; shift += 5 } while (b >= 0x20)
32:     lng += (result & 1) !== 0 ? ~(result >> 1) : result >> 1
33:     poly.push({ lat: lat / 1e5, lng: lng / 1e5 })
34:   }
35:   return poly
36: }
37: 
38: function distanceToPolyline(stopLat: number, stopLng: number, polyline: Array<{ lat: number; lng: number }>): { distanceKm: number; cumulativeKm: number } {
39:   let minDistance = Infinity
40:   let bestCumulative = 0
41:   let runningDistance = 0
42:   for (let i = 0; i < polyline.length; i++) {
43:     const d = calculateDistance(stopLat, stopLng, polyline[i].lat, polyline[i].lng)
44:     if (d < minDistance) { minDistance = d; bestCumulative = runningDistance }
45:     if (i < polyline.length - 1) {
46:       runningDistance += calculateDistance(polyline[i].lat, polyline[i].lng, polyline[i + 1].lat, polyline[i + 1].lng)
47:     }
48:   }
49:   return { distanceKm: minDistance, cumulativeKm: bestCumulative }
50: }
51: 
52: function buildDirectionsUrl(params: {
53:   startLat: number
54:   startLng: number
55:   destLat: number
56:   destLng: number
57:   apiKey: string
58:   rigType?: string | null
59:   avoidGravelRoads?: boolean
60: }) {
61:   const urlParams = new URLSearchParams({
62:     origin: `${params.startLat},${params.startLng}`,
63:     destination: `${params.destLat},${params.destLng}`,
64:     mode: "driving",
65:     key: params.apiKey,
66:   })
67: 
68:   const normalizedRig = (params.rigType || "").toLowerCase()
69:   if (["caravan", "motorhome", "campervan"].includes(normalizedRig)) {
70:     urlParams.append("avoid", "ferries")
71:   }
72: 
73:   if (params.avoidGravelRoads) {
74:     urlParams.append("alternatives", "true")
75:   }
76: 
77:   return `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/directions/json?${urlParams.toString()}`
78: }
79: 
80: function selectBestDirectionsRoute(
81:   routes: Array<{ summary?: string; overview_polyline?: { points: string }; legs: Array<{ distance: { value: number } }> }>,
82:   threshold = 1.1
83: ) {
84:   if (routes.length <= 1) {
85:     return routes[0]
86:   }
87: 
88:   const sortedByDistance = routes
89:     .filter((route) => route.legs?.[0]?.distance?.value)
90:     .slice()
91:     .sort((a, b) => a.legs[0].distance.value - b.legs[0].distance.value)
92: 
93:   if (sortedByDistance.length === 0) {
94:     return routes[0]
95:   }
96: 
97:   const shortestDistance = sortedByDistance[0].legs[0].distance.value
98: 
99:   const highwayPreferred = sortedByDistance.find((route) => {
100:     const summary = (route.summary || "").toLowerCase()
101:     const isHighway = summary.includes("highway") || summary.includes("hwy") || summary.includes("freeway") || summary.includes("motorway")
102:     if (!isHighway) return false
103: 
104:     const ratio = route.legs[0].distance.value / shortestDistance
105:     return ratio <= threshold
106:   })
107: 
108:   return highwayPreferred || sortedByDistance[0]
109: }

149: async function findCorridor(startLat: number, startLng: number, destLat: number, destLng: number): Promise<string | null> {
150:   const corridorCenters = await getCorridorsFromStops()
151: 
152:   if (Object.keys(corridorCenters).length === 0) {
153:     console.log("⚠️ No corridors found in stops table, returning null")
154:     return null
155:   }
156: 
157:   let bestCorridor: string | null = null
158:   let bestScore = Infinity
159: 
160:   for (const [corridor, coords] of Object.entries(corridorCenters)) {
161:     const score = calcDistance(startLat, startLng, coords.lat, coords.lng) +
162:                  calcDistance(destLat, destLng, coords.lat, coords.lng)
163:     if (score < bestScore) {
164:       bestScore = score
165:       bestCorridor = corridor
166:     }
167:   }
168: 
169:   return bestCorridor
170: }

172: function normalizeCorridorName(value: string | null | undefined): string {
173:   return (value || "")
174:     .toLowerCase()
175:     .replace(/highway|hwy|road|route/g, "")
176:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "")
177: }

179: function corridorMatchesStop(stopCorridor: string | null | undefined, detectedCorridor: string | null): boolean {
180:   if (!detectedCorridor || detectedCorridor === "Unknown") return true
181:   if (!stopCorridor) return true
182: 
183:   const detected = normalizeCorridorName(detectedCorridor)
184:   const stop = normalizeCorridorName(stopCorridor)
185:   if (!detected || !stop) return true
186: 
187:   return stop === detected || stop.includes(detected) || detected.includes(stop)
188: }
189: 
190: function interpolatePoint(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number, fraction: number) {
191:   return {
192:     lat: lat1 + (lat2 - lat1) * fraction,
193:     lng: lng1 + (lng2 - lng1) * fraction,
194:   }
195: }

262: async function fetchGoogleRelatedStops(startLat: number, startLng: number, destLat: number, destLng: number): Promise<GoogleRelatedStop[]> {
263:   const apiKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GMAPS_API_KEY
264:   if (!apiKey) return []
265: 
266:   const probeFractions = [0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8]
267:   const searchTypes = ["gas_station", "campground", "supermarket"]
268:   const dedupe = new Set<string>()
269:   const results: GoogleRelatedStop[] = []
270: 
271:   for (const fraction of probeFractions) {
272:     const point = interpolatePoint(startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng, fraction)
273: 
274:     for (const type of searchTypes) {
275:       try {
276:         const url = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/nearbysearch/json?location=${point.lat},${point.lng}&radius=${env.NEARBY_STOPS_SEARCH_RADIUS}&type=${type}&key=${apiKey}`
277:         const response = await fetch(url)
278:         const data = await response.json()
279: 
280:         const places = Array.isArray(data?.results) ? data.results : []
281:         for (const place of places.slice(0, 5) as Array<{
282:           name?: string
283:           vicinity?: string
284:           geometry?: { location?: { lat?: number; lng?: number } }
285:           types?: string[]
286:         }>) {
287:           const lat = place.geometry?.location?.lat
288:           const lng = place.geometry?.location?.lng
289:           const name = place.name?.trim()
290:           if (!name || typeof lat !== "number" || typeof lng !== "number") continue
291: 
292:           const placeType = Array.isArray(place.types) && place.types.length > 0 ? place.types[0] : type
293:           const key = `${name.toLowerCase()}::${lat.toFixed(4)}::${lng.toFixed(4)}`
294:           if (dedupe.has(key)) continue
295:           dedupe.add(key)
296: 
297:           results.push({
298:             name,
299:             latitude: String(lat),
300:             longitude: String(lng),
301:             address: place.vicinity || "",
302:             place_type: placeType,
303:           })
304:         }
305:       } catch (error) {
306:         console.error("Error fetching Google related stops:", error)
307:       }
308:     }
309:   }
310: 
311:   return results
312: }

314: export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
315:   try {
316:     if (!supabaseAdmin) {
317:       return NextResponse.json(
318:         { success: false, error: "Database not configured" },
319:         { status: 500 }
320:       )
321:     }
322: 
323:     const body = await req.json()
324:     const {
325:       tripId,
326:       startLat,
327:       startLng,
328:       destLat,
329:       destLng,
330:     } = body
331: 
332:     if (!tripId || !startLat || !startLng || !destLat || !destLng) {
333:       return NextResponse.json(
334:         { success: false, error: "tripId and coordinates are required" },
335:         { status: 400 }
336:       )
337:     }
338: 
339:     const corridor = await findCorridor(startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng)
340: 
341:     // Fetch trip preferences for suitability filtering (non-fatal if missing)
342:     let tripPreferences: TripPreferences | null = null
343:     const { data: tripData, error: tripError } = await supabaseAdmin
344:       .from("trips")
345:       .select("rig_type, rig_length_m, pet_friendly_required, avoid_gravel_roads, stay_preference, budget_preference, end_date, trip_duration_days")
346:       .eq("id", tripId)
347:       .single()
348:     if (!tripError && tripData) {
349:       tripPreferences = tripData as TripPreferences
350:     }
351: 
352:     console.log("\n" + "=".repeat(60))
353:     console.log("🔍 FINDING STOPS ALONG ROUTE")
354:     console.log("=".repeat(60))
355:     console.log(`📍 Start Coordinates: ${startLat}, ${startLng}`)
356:     console.log(`📍 Destination Coordinates: ${destLat}, ${destLng}`)
357:     console.log(`🛣️  Detected Primary Corridor: ${corridor}`)
358:     console.log("=".repeat(60))
359: 
360:     // Create bounding box to catch stops across multiple corridors
361:     const bounds = {
362:       minLat: Math.min(startLat, destLat) - 1,
363:       maxLat: Math.max(startLat, destLat) + 1,
364:       minLng: Math.min(startLng, destLng) - 1,
365:       maxLng: Math.max(startLng, destLng) + 1,
366:     }
367: 
368:     console.log(`📦 Bounding Box for search:`)
369:     console.log(`   Min Lat: ${bounds.minLat.toFixed(2)}, Max Lat: ${bounds.maxLat.toFixed(2)}`)
370:     console.log(`   Min Lng: ${bounds.minLng.toFixed(2)}, Max Lng: ${bounds.maxLng.toFixed(2)}`)
371: 
372:     // Fetch all stops in bounding box (not just corridor)
373:     const { data: stops, error: stopsError } = await supabaseAdmin
374:       .from("stops")
375:       .select("*")
376:       .gte("latitude", bounds.minLat.toString())
377:       .lte("latitude", bounds.maxLat.toString())
378:       .gte("longitude", bounds.minLng.toString())
379:       .lte("longitude", bounds.maxLng.toString())
380: 
381:     console.log(`✅ Database Query Result: ${stops?.length || 0} stops found in bounding box`)
382: 
383:     if (stopsError) {
384:       console.error("❌ Error fetching stops:", stopsError)
385:       return NextResponse.json(
386:         { success: false, error: stopsError.message },
387:         { status: 500 }
388:       )
389:     }
390: 
391:     if (!stops || stops.length === 0) {
392:       console.log("⚠️  No stops found in bounding box!")
393:       console.log(`   You may need to expand search area or check if stops table has data`)
394:       return NextResponse.json({
395:         success: true,
396:         stops: [],
397:         route: [[startLat, startLng], [destLat, destLng]],
398:         corridor: corridor,
399:         bounds: bounds,
400:         message: "No stops found in search area",
401:       })
402:     }
403: 
404:     console.log(`\n📋 Fetched ${stops.length} stops, filtering by route proximity...`)
405: 
406:     // Attempt to get the actual route polyline from Google Directions for precise proximity filtering
407:     const polyline = await fetchRoutePolylineWithPreferences(startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng, tripPreferences)
408:     const usingPolyline = polyline !== null && polyline.length > 2
409:     console.log(usingPolyline
410:       ? `🗺️  Using actual route polyline (${polyline!.length} points) — threshold: ${ROUTE_PROXIMITY_THRESHOLD_KM}km`
411:       : `⚠️  No polyline available — falling back to straight-line triangle inequality`
412:     )
413: 
414:     const directDistance = calculateDistance(startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng)
415: 
416:     const stopsWithDistance = stops
417:       .map((stop) => {
418:         const lat = parseFloat(stop.latitude)
419:         const lng = parseFloat(stop.longitude)
420: 
421:         let isBetween: boolean
422:         let distanceToRoute: number
423:         let cumulativeKm = 0
424: 
425:         if (usingPolyline) {
426:           const { distanceKm, cumulativeKm: cum } = distanceToPolyline(lat, lng, polyline!)
427:           distanceToRoute = distanceKm
428:           cumulativeKm = cum
429:           isBetween = distanceKm <= ROUTE_PROXIMITY_THRESHOLD_KM
430:         } else {
431:           const distFromStart = calculateDistance(startLat, startLng, lat, lng)
432:           const distFromDest = calculateDistance(destLat, destLng, lat, lng)
433:           distanceToRoute = Math.min(distFromStart, distFromDest)
434:           cumulativeKm = distFromStart
435:           isBetween = (distFromStart + distFromDest) <= (directDistance + 50)
436:         }
437: 
438:         return {
439:           ...stop,
440:           distance_from_start_km: cumulativeKm,
441:           distance_to_route_km: distanceToRoute,

77: function calculateDistance(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number) {
78:   const R = 6371
79:   const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180
80:   const dLng = (lng2 - lng1) * Math.PI / 180
81:   const a =
82:     Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +
83:     Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) *
84:     Math.sin(dLng / 2) * Math.sin(dLng / 2)
85:   const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a))
86:   return R * c
87: }

89: async function findCorridor(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number): Promise<string> {
90:   const corridorCenters = await getCorridorsFromStops()
91:   
92:   const corridorNames = Object.keys(corridorCenters)
93:   if (corridorNames.length === 0) {
94:     return "Unknown"
95:   }
96: 
97:   let closestCorridor = corridorNames[0]
98:   let minDistance = Infinity
99:   const midLat = (lat1 + lat2) / 2
100:   const midLng = (lng1 + lng2) / 2
101: 
102:   for (const [name, coords] of Object.entries(corridorCenters)) {
103:     const distance = calcDistance(midLat, midLng, coords.lat, coords.lng)
104:     if (distance < minDistance) {
105:       minDistance = distance
106:       closestCorridor = name
107:     }
108:   }
109: 
110:   return closestCorridor
111: }

113: function normalizeCorridorName(value: string | null | undefined): string {
114:   return (value || "")
115:     .toLowerCase()
116:     .replace(/highway|hwy|road|route/g, "")
117:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "")
118: }

120: function corridorMatchesStop(stopCorridor: string | null | undefined, detectedCorridor: string): boolean {
121:   if (!detectedCorridor || detectedCorridor === "Unknown") return true
122:   if (!stopCorridor) return true
123: 
124:   const detected = normalizeCorridorName(detectedCorridor)
125:   const stop = normalizeCorridorName(stopCorridor)
126:   if (!detected || !stop) return true
127: 
128:   return stop === detected || stop.includes(detected) || detected.includes(stop)
129: }

131: function buildDirectionsUrl(params: {
132:   startLat: number
133:   startLng: number
134:   destLat: number
135:   destLng: number
136:   apiKey: string
137:   rigType?: string | null

142:     destination: `${params.destLat},${params.destLng}`,
143:     mode: "driving",
144:     key: params.apiKey,
145:   })
146: 
147:   const normalizedRig = (params.rigType || "").toLowerCase()
148:   if (["caravan", "motorhome", "campervan"].includes(normalizedRig)) {
149:     urlParams.append("avoid", "ferries")
150:   }
151:

153:     urlParams.append("alternatives", "true")
154:   }

159: function selectBestDirectionsRoute(routes: GoogleDirectionsRoute[], threshold = 1.1) {
160:   if (routes.length <= 1) {
161:     return routes[0]
162:   }
163: 
164:   const sortedByDistance = routes
165:     .filter((route) => route.legs?.[0]?.distance?.value)
166:     .slice()
167:     .sort((a, b) => a.legs[0].distance.value - b.legs[0].distance.value)
168: 
169:   if (sortedByDistance.length === 0) {
170:     return routes[0]
171:   }
172: 
173:   const shortestDistance = sortedByDistance[0].legs[0].distance.value
174: 
175:   const highwayPreferred = sortedByDistance.find((route) => {
176:     const summary = (route.summary || "").toLowerCase()
177:     const isHighway = summary.includes("highway") || summary.includes("hwy") || summary.includes("freeway") || summary.includes("motorway")
178:     if (!isHighway) return false
179: 
180:     const ratio = route.legs[0].distance.value / shortestDistance
181:     return ratio <= threshold
182:   })
183: 
184:   return highwayPreferred || sortedByDistance[0]
185: }

187: function interpolatePoint(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number, fraction: number) {
188:   return {
189:     lat: lat1 + (lat2 - lat1) * fraction,
190:     lng: lng1 + (lng2 - lng1) * fraction,
191:   }
192: }

194: function projectDistanceAlongRouteKm(params: {
195:   startLat: number
196:   startLng: number
197:   destLat: number
198:   destLng: number
199:   pointLat: number
200:   pointLng: number
201:   totalDistanceKm: number
202: }) {
203:   const {
204:     startLat,
205:     startLng,
206:     destLat,
207:     destLng,
208:     pointLat,
209:     pointLng,
210:     totalDistanceKm,
211:   } = params
212: 
213:   const effectiveTotalKm = Number.isFinite(totalDistanceKm) && totalDistanceKm > 0
214:     ? totalDistanceKm
215:     : calculateDistance(startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng)
216: 
217:   if (!Number.isFinite(effectiveTotalKm) || effectiveTotalKm <= 0) return 0
218: 
219:   // Use an equirectangular projection for stable local vector math.
220:   const avgLatRad = ((startLat + destLat) / 2) * Math.PI / 180
221:   const scaleX = Math.cos(avgLatRad)
222: 
223:   const sx = startLng * scaleX
224:   const sy = startLat
225:   const dx = destLng * scaleX
226:   const dy = destLat
227:   const px = pointLng * scaleX
228:   const py = pointLat
229: 
230:   const vx = dx - sx
231:   const vy = dy - sy
232:   const wx = px - sx
233:   const wy = py - sy
234:   const vLenSq = (vx * vx) + (vy * vy)
235: 
236:   if (!Number.isFinite(vLenSq) || vLenSq <= 1e-12) {
237:     return Math.round(calculateDistance(startLat, startLng, pointLat, pointLng) * 10) / 10
238:   }
239: 
240:   const tRaw = ((wx * vx) + (wy * vy)) / vLenSq
241:   const t = clamp(tRaw, 0, 1)
242: 
243:   return Math.round(effectiveTotalKm * t * 10) / 10
244: }

246: function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
247:   return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value))
248: }

252: function getFuelSafetyConfig(northWeight: number, isRemote: boolean) {
253:   const riskWeight = clamp(Math.max(northWeight, isRemote ? 0.65 : 0), 0, 1)
254:   const fuelSafeKm = clamp(230 - riskWeight * 120, 90, 230)
255:   const leadKm = clamp(45 + riskWeight * 60, 45, 120)
256: 
257:   return { fuelSafeKm, leadKm }

260: function buildAdaptiveBoundaries(
261:   totalDistanceKm: number,
262:   baseTargetKm: number,
263:   baseMinKm: number,
264:   baseMaxKm: number,
265:   stopDistances: number[],
266:   northbound: boolean
267: ) {
268:   const boundaries: Array<{ startKm: number; endKm: number }> = []
269:   let current = 0
270:   let guard = 0
271: 
272:   while (current < totalDistanceKm - 5 && guard < 40) {
273:     guard += 1
274:     const progress = totalDistanceKm > 0 ? current / totalDistanceKm : 0
275:     const northWeight = northbound ? clamp((progress - 0.55) * 2.2, 0, 1) : 0
276: 
277:     const targetKm = baseTargetKm - northWeight * 25
278:     const minKm = clamp(baseMinKm - northWeight * 10, 100, 260)
279:     const maxKm = clamp(baseMaxKm + northWeight * 40, 220, 380)
280: 
281:     const remaining = totalDistanceKm - current
282:     if (remaining <= maxKm * 1.15) {
283:       boundaries.push({ startKm: current, endKm: totalDistanceKm })
284:       break
285:     }
286: 
287:     let endKm = current + targetKm
288:     const windowStart = current + minKm
289:     const windowEnd = current + maxKm
290:     const windowStops = stopDistances.filter((d) => d >= windowStart && d <= windowEnd)
291: 
292:     if (windowStops.length > 0) {
293:       const nearestToTarget = windowStops.reduce((best, value) => {
294:         if (best === null) return value
295:         return Math.abs(value - endKm) < Math.abs(best - endKm) ? value : best
296:       }, null as number | null)
297:       endKm = clamp(nearestToTarget ?? endKm, windowStart, windowEnd)
298:     } else {
299:       const extraStretch = northWeight > 0.2 ? 45 : 30
300:       endKm = clamp(current + maxKm + extraStretch, current + minKm, totalDistanceKm)
301:     }
302: 
303:     boundaries.push({ startKm: current, endKm })
304:     current = endKm
305:   }
306: 
307:   if (boundaries.length === 0) {
308:     boundaries.push({ startKm: 0, endKm: totalDistanceKm })
309:   }
310: 
311:   return boundaries
312: }
313: 
314: function rankStops(
315:   stops: RouteStopCandidate[],
316:   segmentMidKm: number,
317:   preferredCount: number,
318:   preferVerified?: boolean
319: ) {
320:   return stops
321:     .slice()
322:     .sort((a, b) => {
323:       const aVerifiedBonus = preferVerified && a.is_verified ? -50 : 0
324:       const bVerifiedBonus = preferVerified && b.is_verified ? -50 : 0
325:       const aScore = Math.abs(a.distance_from_start_km - segmentMidKm) + (a.stay_type ? 0 : 25) + aVerifiedBonus
326:       const bScore = Math.abs(b.distance_from_start_km - segmentMidKm) + (b.stay_type ? 0 : 25) + bVerifiedBonus
327:       return aScore - bScore
328:     })
329:     .slice(0, preferredCount)
330: }

390:     // Decoded polyline from Google Directions — used for accurate stop positioning
391:     // and fuel probe generation instead of straight-line approximations.
392:     let routePolyline: PolylinePoint[] | null = null
393:     let routeCumTable: number[] | null = null
394:

407:         const directionsData = await directionsResponse.json()
408:

410:           const selectedRoute = selectBestDirectionsRoute(directionsData.routes as GoogleDirectionsRoute[])
411:           const route = selectedRoute?.legs?.[0]

414:           }

418:           }
419:           // Extract and decode the polyline for accurate geometric operations.
420:           const encodedPolyline = selectedRoute?.overview_polyline?.points
421:           if (encodedPolyline) {
422:             routePolyline = decodePolyline(encodedPolyline)
423:             routeCumTable = buildCumulativeDistanceTable(routePolyline)
424:           }
425:         }
426:       } catch (err) {
427:         console.error("Error fetching directions:", err)
428:       }
429:     }
430: 
431:     const directDistance = calculateDistance(startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng)
432: 
433:     // Dynamic bounding box: scale buffer with route distance
434:     // ~1 degree ≈ 111 km, so for 1956 km route we need ~9° buffer
435:     const bufferDeg = Math.max(2, (directDistance / 111) * 0.3)
436: 
437:     const bounds = {
438:       minLat: Math.min(startLat, destLat) - bufferDeg,
439:       maxLat: Math.max(startLat, destLat) + bufferDeg,
440:       minLng: Math.min(startLng, destLng) - bufferDeg,
441:       maxLng: Math.max(startLng, destLng) + bufferDeg,
442:     }
443: 
444:     const { data: stops, error: stopsError } = await supabaseAdmin
445:       .from("stops")
446:       .select("*")
447:       .gte("latitude", bounds.minLat)
448:       .lte("latitude", bounds.maxLat)
449:       .gte("longitude", bounds.minLng)
450:       .lte("longitude", bounds.maxLng)
451: 
452:     if (stopsError) {
453:       return NextResponse.json(
454:         { success: false, error: stopsError.message },
455:         { status: 500 }
456:       )
457:     }
458: 
459:     const includeAlternativeStops = includeAlternatives !== false
460:     let customStops: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = []
461: 
462:     if (includeAlternativeStops && tripId) {
463:       const { data: customStopsData, error: customStopsError } = await supabaseAdmin
464:         .from("custom_stops")
465:         .select("*")
466:         .eq("trip_id", tripId)
467: 
468:       if (customStopsError) {
469:         console.error("Error fetching custom stops:", customStopsError)
470:       } else {
471:         customStops = (customStopsData || []).map((stop) => ({
472:           ...stop,
473:           id: `custom-${String(stop.id)}`,
474:           corridor: null,
475:           state: "",
476:           region: "",
477:           route_type: String(stop.place_type || "custom"),
478:           stay_type: "",
479:           pet_friendly: "",
480:           water: "",
481:           cost_band: "",
482:           tier: "",
483:           verification_status: "custom",
484:           is_alternative: true,
485:         }))
486:       }
487:     }
488: 
489:     const allStops = [...(stops || []), ...customStops]
490: 
491:     const routeFiltered = allStops.map((stop) => {
492:       const lat = typeof stop.latitude === "number" ? stop.latitude : parseFloat(stop.latitude)
493:       const lng = typeof stop.longitude === "number" ? stop.longitude : parseFloat(stop.longitude)
494:       const isVerified = Boolean(stop.verification_status && stop.verification_status !== "custom")
495: 
496:       let distanceFromStartKm: number
497:       let distanceFromDestKm: number
498:       let lateralKm: number
499:       let isBetween: boolean
500: 
501:       if (routePolyline && routeCumTable && routeCumTable.length > 0) {
502:         // --- Polyline-based projection (accurate for curved routes) ---
503:         const totalRouteKm = routeCumTable[routeCumTable.length - 1]
504:         const { distanceFromStartKm: routeDistKm, lateralKm: routeLateralKm } =
505:           projectPointOntoPolyline(lat, lng, routePolyline, routeCumTable)
506: 
507:         distanceFromStartKm = routeDistKm
508:         distanceFromDestKm = Math.max(0, totalRouteKm - routeDistKm)
509:         lateralKm = routeLateralKm
510: 
511:         // A stop is on the route if it is within the lateral threshold of the actual
512:         // road and its projection falls between start (−5%) and end (+5%) of total
513:         // route length.
514:         // Verified (curated) database stops: 100 km threshold — some verified stops
515:         // may be deliberately accessed via a short side road.
516:         // Custom/unverified Google Places stops: 25 km threshold — prevents offshore
517:         // islands (e.g. Whitsunday Island ~31 km from the Bruce Highway), island
518:         // resorts, and other car-inaccessible places from appearing.
519:         const lateralThreshold = isVerified ? 100 : 25
520:         const tolerance = totalRouteKm * 0.05
521:         isBetween = lateralKm <= lateralThreshold &&
522:           distanceFromStartKm >= -tolerance &&
523:           distanceFromStartKm <= totalRouteKm + tolerance
524:       } else {
525:         // --- Straight-line fallback (no polyline available) ---
526:         const distFromStart = calculateDistance(startLat, startLng, lat, lng)
527:         const distFromDest = calculateDistance(destLat, destLng, lat, lng)
528:         const sumDist = distFromStart + distFromDest
529:         const isBetweenEllipse = sumDist <= (directDistance + Math.min(config.maxSpacing, 120))
530: 
531:         const avgLatRad = ((startLat + destLat) / 2) * Math.PI / 180
532:         const scaleX = Math.cos(avgLatRad)
533:         const vx = (destLng - startLng) * scaleX
534:         const vy = destLat - startLat
535:         const wx = (lng - startLng) * scaleX
536:         const wy = lat - startLat
537:         const vLenSq = vx * vx + vy * vy
538:         const tRaw = vLenSq > 1e-12 ? (wx * vx + wy * vy) / vLenSq : 0
539:         const isForwardOnRoute = tRaw >= -0.05 && tRaw <= 1.05
540:         const tClamped = clamp(tRaw, 0, 1)
541:         const projLat = startLat + tClamped * (destLat - startLat)
542:         const projLng = startLng + tClamped * (destLng - startLng)
543: 
544:         distanceFromStartKm = Math.round(distFromStart * 10) / 10
545:         distanceFromDestKm = Math.round(distFromDest * 10) / 10
546:         lateralKm = calculateDistance(lat, lng, projLat, projLng)
547:         const lateralThresholdFallback = isVerified ? 100 : 25
548:         isBetween = isBetweenEllipse && isForwardOnRoute && lateralKm <= lateralThresholdFallback
549:       }
550: 
551:       return {
552:         ...stop,
553:         distance_from_start_km: Math.round(distanceFromStartKm * 10) / 10,
554:         distance_from_dest_km: Math.round(distanceFromDestKm * 10) / 10,
555:         is_between: isBetween,
556:         is_verified: isVerified,
557:       }
558:     }).filter((stop) => stop.is_between)
559:     .sort((a, b) => a.distance_from_start_km - b.distance_from_start_km)
560: 
561:     // Verified stops (from the stops table) always pass the corridor check — their
562:     // corridor field often won't match the 6 coarse auto-detected corridors (e.g.
563:     // "Stuart Highway" vs "Outback"), so filtering them would silently drop real stops.
564:     const corridorFiltered = routeFiltered.filter((stop) =>
565:       stop.is_verified || corridorMatchesStop(stop.corridor, corridor)
566:     )
567: 
568:     const stopsWithDistance = (tripPreferences
569:       ? applySuitabilityFilter(corridorFiltered, tripPreferences)
570:       : corridorFiltered) as RouteStopCandidate[]
571: 
572:     const fuelStationMap = new Map<string, FuelStationOption>()
573: 
574:     const segments: PlannedSegment[] = []
575:     // Tracks stops that have been the primary overnight anchor — these are excluded
576:     // from being the anchor again on another day, but can still appear as options.
577:     const anchoredStopIds = new Set<string>()
578: 
579:     const totalDistanceKm = drivingInfo?.totalDistanceKm ?? directDistance
580:     const suggestedDays = Math.max(1, Math.round(totalDistanceKm / config.kmPerDay))
581:     const planningDays = requestedTripDays && requestedTripDays > 0
582:       ? requestedTripDays
583:       : suggestedDays
584:     const northbound = destLat > startLat
585:     const latSpan = Math.abs(destLat - startLat)
586:     const remoteMultiplier = northbound && latSpan > 5 ? 0.75 : 1.0
587:     const targetLegKm = clamp(
588:       (preferredLegKm ?? config.kmPerDay) * remoteMultiplier,
589:       env.LEG_TARGET_MIN_KM,
590:       avoidLongDays ? env.AVOID_LONG_DAYS_KM : env.DEFAULT_MAX_KM_PER_DAY
591:     )
592:     const minLegKm = clamp(targetLegKm - env.LEG_SPACING_KM, env.LEG_MIN_KM, 220)
593:     const maxLegKm = clamp(
594:       targetLegKm + env.LEG_SPACING_KM,
595:       env.LEG_MAX_KM,
596:       avoidLongDays ? env.LEG_AVOID_LONG_DAYS_ADD_KM + env.AVOID_LONG_DAYS_KM : 450
597:     )
598: 
599:     // When the polyline is available, stop distances are already accurate driving-km
600:     // values (from projectPointOntoPolyline). The linear scale factor is only needed
601:     // as a fallback when we have only the haversine distance.
602:     const scaledStopsWithDistance: RouteStopCandidate[] = (() => {
603:       if (routePolyline && routeCumTable) {
604:         // Polyline path: distances are already in actual driving km — no scaling needed.
605:         return stopsWithDistance
606:       }
607:       // Fallback: scale haversine distances proportionally to actual driving distance.
608:       const scaleFactor = totalDistanceKm > 0 && directDistance > 0
609:         ? totalDistanceKm / directDistance
610:         : 1
611:       return scaleFactor !== 1
612:         ? stopsWithDistance.map((s) => ({
613:             ...s,
614:             distance_from_start_km: Math.round(s.distance_from_start_km * scaleFactor * 10) / 10,
615:           }))
616:         : stopsWithDistance
617:     })()
618: 
619:     const stopDistances = scaledStopsWithDistance.map((s) => s.distance_from_start_km)
620: 
621:     let boundaries = buildAdaptiveBoundaries(
622:       totalDistanceKm,
623:       targetLegKm,
624:       minLegKm,
625:       maxLegKm,
626:       stopDistances,
627:       northbound
628:     )
629: 
630:     // Reconcile adaptive boundary count with planned day count.
631:     // Only override with equal-length segments when the adaptive algorithm is more
632:     // than 2× off from the user's requested day count. Otherwise keep the adaptive
633:     // boundaries (which respect stop clusters and remote stretches) and trim/extend.
634:     if (planningDays > 0 && boundaries.length !== planningDays) {
635:       const ratio = boundaries.length / planningDays
636: 
637:       if (ratio < 0.5 || ratio > 2.0) {
638:         // Adaptive result is too far off — fall back to equal split.
639:         const equalBoundaries: Array<{ startKm: number; endKm: number }> = []
640:         const segmentLength = totalDistanceKm / planningDays
641:         for (let i = 0; i < planningDays; i++) {
642:           const startKm = i * segmentLength
643:           const endKm = i === planningDays - 1 ? totalDistanceKm : (i + 1) * segmentLength
644:           equalBoundaries.push({
645:             startKm: Math.round(startKm * 10) / 10,
646:             endKm: Math.round(endKm * 10) / 10,
647:           })
648:         }
649:         boundaries = equalBoundaries
650:       } else if (boundaries.length > planningDays) {
651:         // Merge the shortest adjacent pair until we reach planningDays.
652:         while (boundaries.length > planningDays) {
653:           let minIdx = 0
654:           let minLen = boundaries[0].endKm - boundaries[0].startKm
655:           for (let i = 1; i < boundaries.length; i++) {
656:             const len = boundaries[i].endKm - boundaries[i].startKm
657:             if (len < minLen) { minLen = len; minIdx = i }
658:           }
659:           const mergeWith = minIdx === 0 ? 1 : minIdx - 1
660:           const lo = Math.min(minIdx, mergeWith)
661:           const hi = Math.max(minIdx, mergeWith)
662:           boundaries.splice(lo, 2, {
663:             startKm: boundaries[lo].startKm,
664:             endKm: boundaries[hi].endKm,
665:           })
666:         }
667:       } else {
668:         // Need more days — split the longest segment.
669:         while (boundaries.length < planningDays) {
670:           let maxIdx = 0
671:           let maxLen = boundaries[0].endKm - boundaries[0].startKm
672:           for (let i = 1; i < boundaries.length; i++) {
673:             const len = boundaries[i].endKm - boundaries[i].startKm
674:             if (len > maxLen) { maxLen = len; maxIdx = i }
675:           }
676:           const { startKm, endKm } = boundaries[maxIdx]
677:           const mid = Math.round(((startKm + endKm) / 2) * 10) / 10
678:           boundaries.splice(maxIdx, 1,
679:             { startKm, endKm: mid },
680:             { startKm: mid, endKm }
681:           )
682:         }
683:       }
684:     }
685: 
686:     for (let i = 0; i < boundaries.length; i++) {
687:       const startKm = boundaries[i].startKm
688:       const endKm = boundaries[i].endKm
689:       const midKm = (startKm + endKm) / 2
690:       const progress = totalDistanceKm > 0 ? midKm / totalDistanceKm : 0
691:       const northWeight = northbound ? clamp((progress - 0.55) * 2.2, 0, 1) : 0
692: 
693:       const MIN_STOPS_PER_SEGMENT = 2
694:       const TARGET_STOPS_PER_SEGMENT = 3
695: 
696:       const inRange = scaledStopsWithDistance.filter(
697:         (s) => s.distance_from_start_km >= (startKm - 35) && s.distance_from_start_km <= (endKm + 35)
698:       )
699: 
700:       const expandedInRange = scaledStopsWithDistance.filter(
701:         (s) => s.distance_from_start_km >= (startKm - 75) && s.distance_from_start_km <= (endKm + 75)
702:       )
703: 
704:       // Only exclude stops that have already been anchored as a primary overnight pick.
705:       // This allows a stop to still appear as an *option* for nearby segments even after
706:       // being anchored, which keeps the option count healthy in sparse regions.
707:       const inRangeUnused = inRange.filter((s) => !anchoredStopIds.has(s.id))
708: 
709:       const expandedUnused = expandedInRange.filter((s) => !anchoredStopIds.has(s.id))
710:       const isForwardEnough = (s: RouteStopCandidate) => s.distance_from_start_km >= (startKm - 20)
711: 
712:       const canIncludeAsOther = (s: RouteStopCandidate) => s.is_verified || includeFreeCamps !== false || s.cost_band !== "Free"
713: 
714:       const nearestUnusedBySegment = rankStops(
715:         expandedUnused.filter((s) => canIncludeAsOther(s)),
716:         midKm,
717:         14,
718:         preferVerified
719:       )
720: 
721:       let verifiedInSegment = rankStops(
722:         inRangeUnused.filter((s) => s.is_verified),
723:         midKm,
724:         6,
725:         preferVerified
726:       )
727:       let otherInSegment = rankStops(
728:         inRangeUnused.filter((s) => !s.is_verified && canIncludeAsOther(s)),
729:         midKm,
730:         8
731:       )
732: 
733:       let degradedMode = false
734: 
735:       const totalCandidates = () => verifiedInSegment.length + otherInSegment.length
736: 
737:       const appendCandidates = (candidates: RouteStopCandidate[]) => {
738:         for (const candidate of candidates) {
739:           if (verifiedInSegment.some((v) => v.id === candidate.id) || otherInSegment.some((o) => o.id === candidate.id)) {
740:             continue
741:           }
742:           if (candidate.is_verified) {
743:             verifiedInSegment.push(candidate)
744:           } else if (canIncludeAsOther(candidate)) {
745:             otherInSegment.push(candidate)
746:           }
747:           if (totalCandidates() >= TARGET_STOPS_PER_SEGMENT) {
748:             break
749:           }
750:         }
751:       }
752: 
753:       if (verifiedInSegment.length < 1) {
754:         const fallbackVerified = rankStops(
755:           expandedUnused.filter((s) => s.is_verified),
756:           midKm,
757:           12,
758:           preferVerified
759:         ).filter((s) => !verifiedInSegment.some((v) => v.id === s.id))
760:         verifiedInSegment = [...verifiedInSegment, ...fallbackVerified].slice(0, 6)
761:       }
762: 
763:       // Trigger fallback earlier to keep 2-3 options available for each segment.
764:       if (totalCandidates() < TARGET_STOPS_PER_SEGMENT) {
765:         appendCandidates(nearestUnusedBySegment)
766:       }
767: 
768:       if (totalCandidates() < MIN_STOPS_PER_SEGMENT) {
769:         const nearestGlobalUnused = rankStops(
770:           scaledStopsWithDistance.filter((s) => !anchoredStopIds.has(s.id) && canIncludeAsOther(s) && isForwardEnough(s)),
771:           midKm,
772:           20,
773:           preferVerified
774:         )
775:         appendCandidates(nearestGlobalUnused)
776:       }
777: 
778:       if (totalCandidates() < MIN_STOPS_PER_SEGMENT) {
779:         // Prefer stops not yet used as overnight anchors to avoid day-to-day repeats
780:         const nearestGlobal = rankStops(
781:           scaledStopsWithDistance.filter((s) => canIncludeAsOther(s) && !anchoredStopIds.has(s.id) && isForwardEnough(s)),
782:           midKm,
783:           20,
784:           preferVerified
785:         )
786:         appendCandidates(nearestGlobal)
787:       }
788: 
789:       if (totalCandidates() === 0) {
790:         degradedMode = true
791:         const lookaheadKm = Math.max(220, config.kmPerDay)
792:         const forwardFallback = rankStops(
793:           scaledStopsWithDistance.filter(
794:             (s) =>
795:               canIncludeAsOther(s) &&
796:               !anchoredStopIds.has(s.id) &&
797:               s.distance_from_start_km >= (startKm - 20) &&
798:               s.distance_from_start_km <= (endKm + lookaheadKm)
799:           ),
800:           endKm + lookaheadKm * 0.5,
801:           3,
802:           preferVerified
803:         )
804: 
805:         if (forwardFallback.length > 0) {
806:           appendCandidates(forwardFallback)
807:         }
808: 
809:         // Final rescue: if end-of-route segment is still empty, borrow the nearest
810:         // unanchored stop from adjacent distance bands (slight backtrack allowed).
811:         if (totalCandidates() === 0) {
812:           const backtrackKm = Math.max(140, config.kmPerDay * 0.6)
813:           const nearbyFallback = rankStops(
814:             scaledStopsWithDistance.filter(
815:               (s) =>
816:                 canIncludeAsOther(s) &&
817:                 !anchoredStopIds.has(s.id) &&
818:                 s.distance_from_start_km >= (startKm - backtrackKm) &&
819:                 s.distance_from_start_km <= (endKm + Math.max(80, config.kmPerDay * 0.4))
820:             ),
821:             endKm,
822:             2,
823:             preferVerified
824:           )
825: 
826:           if (nearbyFallback.length > 0) {
827:             appendCandidates(nearbyFallback)
828:           }
829:         }
830: 
831:         if (totalCandidates() === 0) {
832:           // Still no viable forward candidates in this corridor.
833:           verifiedInSegment = []
834:           otherInSegment = []
835:         }
836:       }
837: 
838:       if (verifiedInSegment.length > 6) {
839:         verifiedInSegment = verifiedInSegment.slice(0, 6)
840:       }
841:       if (otherInSegment.length > 8) {
842:         otherInSegment = otherInSegment.slice(0, 8)
843:       }
844: 
845:       const overnightAnchor = verifiedInSegment[0] || otherInSegment[0]
846:       if (overnightAnchor) {
847:         anchoredStopIds.add(overnightAnchor.id)
848:       }
849: 
850:       const segmentDistance = Math.max(0, endKm - startKm)
851:       const remoteByDistance = segmentDistance > (maxLegKm + 30)
852:       const remoteByNorth = northbound && northWeight > 0.42
853:       const remoteBySparseStops = (verifiedInSegment.length + otherInSegment.length) < MIN_STOPS_PER_SEGMENT
854: 
855:       const anchorLat = overnightAnchor
856:         ? parseFloat(String(overnightAnchor.latitude))
857:         : null
858:       const anchorLng = overnightAnchor
859:         ? parseFloat(String(overnightAnchor.longitude))
860:         : null
861: 
862:       segments.push({
863:         startKm,
864:         endKm,
865:         verifiedStops: verifiedInSegment,
866:         otherStops: otherInSegment,
867:         fuelSuggestions: [],
868:         primaryFuelSuggestion: undefined,
869:         isRemote: remoteByDistance || remoteByNorth || remoteBySparseStops,
870:         fuelCritical: false,
871:         degradedMode,
872:         overnightAnchorName: overnightAnchor?.location_name ?? null,
873:         overnightAnchorLat: Number.isFinite(anchorLat) ? anchorLat : null,
874:         overnightAnchorLng: Number.isFinite(anchorLng) ? anchorLng : null,
875:       })
876:     }
877: 
878:     if (apiKey && segments.length > 0) {
879:       const maxProbes = Math.min(segments.length, Math.max(8, Math.ceil(totalDistanceKm / 200)))
880:       const segmentProbeIndexes = Array.from(new Set(
881:         segments.map((_, index) => Math.round(index * (Math.min(segments.length, maxProbes) - 1) / Math.max(1, segments.length - 1)))
882:       ))
883: 
884:       const uniqueProbePoints: Array<{ lat: number; lng: number }> = []
885:       const seenKeys = new Set<string>()
886:       for (const probeIndex of segmentProbeIndexes) {
887:         const segment = segments[Math.min(probeIndex, segments.length - 1)]
888:         if (!segment) continue
889: 
890:         const midKm = (segment.startKm + segment.endKm) / 2
891:         // Use the actual polyline to probe along the real road rather than the
892:         // straight line (which can be in the ocean for coastal routes).
893:         const point = routePolyline && routeCumTable
894:           ? samplePolylineAtKm(midKm, routePolyline, routeCumTable)
895:           : (() => {
896:               const fraction = totalDistanceKm > 0 ? Math.min(0.98, Math.max(0.02, midKm / totalDistanceKm)) : 0.5
897:               return interpolatePoint(startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng, fraction)
898:             })()
899:         const pointKey = `${point.lat.toFixed(3)}:${point.lng.toFixed(3)}`
900: 
901:         if (seenKeys.has(pointKey)) continue
902:         seenKeys.add(pointKey)
903:         uniqueProbePoints.push({ lat: point.lat, lng: point.lng })
904:       }
905: 
906:       const fuelFetchPromises = uniqueProbePoints.map(async ({ lat, lng }) => {
907:         try {
908:           const fuelUrl = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/nearbysearch/json?location=${lat},${lng}&radius=${env.FUEL_STATION_SEARCH_RADIUS}&type=gas_station&key=${apiKey}`
909:           const fuelResponse = await fetch(fuelUrl)
910:           const fuelData = await fuelResponse.json()
911: 
912:           const newStations: FuelStationOption[] = []
913:           if (fuelData.results) {
914:             for (const station of fuelData.results.slice(0, 8) as Array<{
915:               place_id?: string
916:               name: string
917:               geometry: { location: { lat: number; lng: number } }
918:               vicinity?: string
919:               rating?: number
920:               opening_hours?: { open_now?: boolean }
921:             }>) {
922:               const stationLat = station.geometry.location.lat
923:               const stationLng = station.geometry.location.lng
924:               const key = station.place_id || `${stationLat.toFixed(3)}:${stationLng.toFixed(3)}`
925:               const distanceFromStartKm = routePolyline && routeCumTable
926:                 ? projectPointOntoPolyline(stationLat, stationLng, routePolyline, routeCumTable).distanceFromStartKm
927:                 : projectDistanceAlongRouteKm({
928:                     startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng,
929:                     pointLat: stationLat, pointLng: stationLng,
930:                     totalDistanceKm,
931:                   })
932: 
933:               newStations.push({
934:                 id: key,
935:                 name: station.name,
936:                 lat: stationLat,
937:                 lng: stationLng,
938:                 address: station.vicinity || "",
939:                 rating: station.rating,
940:                 isOpenNow: station.opening_hours?.open_now,
941:                 distanceFromStartKm,
942:               })
943:             }
944:           }
945:           return newStations
946:         } catch (err) {
947:           console.error("Error fetching route fuel probe:", err)
948:           return [] as FuelStationOption[]
949:         }
950:       })
951: 
952:       const allInitialStations = await Promise.all(fuelFetchPromises)
953:       for (const stations of allInitialStations.flat()) {
954:         fuelStationMap.set(stations.id, stations)
955:       }
956: 
957:       const allFuelStations = Array.from(fuelStationMap.values()).sort(
958:         (a, b) => a.distanceFromStartKm - b.distanceFromStartKm

961:       // Assign each fuel station to exactly one segment to prevent the same
962:       // station from repeating in adjacent days.  We use strict half-open intervals:
963:       // a station belongs to segment i if startKm[i] <= stationKm < endKm[i]
964:       // (last segment is closed on both ends).  Ties go to the segment whose
965:       // targetFuelKm is closest.
966:       const stationOwner = new Map<string, number>()
967:       for (const station of allFuelStations) {
968:         let bestIdx = -1
969:         let bestDelta = Infinity
970:         for (let i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) {

972:           const isLast = i === segments.length - 1
973:           const inInterval = isLast
974:             ? station.distanceFromStartKm >= seg.startKm && station.distanceFromStartKm <= seg.endKm
975:             : station.distanceFromStartKm >= seg.startKm && station.distanceFromStartKm < seg.endKm

978:           const delta = Math.abs(station.distanceFromStartKm - midKm)
979:           if (delta < bestDelta) { bestDelta = delta; bestIdx = i }
980:         }
981:         // Station falls outside all segment ranges (before start or after end):
982:         // assign to the nearest segment as a forward-lookahead fallback.
983:         if (bestIdx < 0) {
984:           for (let i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) {
985:             const delta = Math.abs(station.distanceFromStartKm - segments[i].endKm)
986:             if (delta < bestDelta) { bestDelta = delta; bestIdx = i }
987:           }
988:         }
989:         if (bestIdx >= 0) stationOwner.set(station.id, bestIdx)

991: 
992:       for (let i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) {

994:         const segmentDistance = Math.max(0, segment.endKm - segment.startKm)
995:         const midKm = (segment.startKm + segment.endKm) / 2
996:         const progress = totalDistanceKm > 0 ? midKm / totalDistanceKm : 0
997:         const northWeight = northbound ? clamp((progress - 0.55) * 2.2, 0, 1) : 0

1004:         const rankedFuel = ownedStations
1005:           .slice()

1008:             const bPenalty = b.isOpenNow === false ? 20 : 0
1009:             const aScore = Math.abs(a.distanceFromStartKm - targetFuelKm) + aPenalty
1010:             const bScore = Math.abs(b.distanceFromStartKm - targetFuelKm) + bPenalty

1015:         // Forward-only lookahead: if this segment has no owned station, look for

1018:         // check — that caused the same station to repeat across multiple days.

1022:                 // Must be within this segment (no cross-day re-use)
1023:                 if (s.distanceFromStartKm < segment.startKm || s.distanceFromStartKm > segment.endKm + 20) return false

1026:                 return owner === undefined || owner === i

1037:       const unfueledSegments = segments.filter((s) => s.fuelSuggestions.length === 0)

1040:         const probedMidpoints = new Set<string>()
1041: 
1042:         for (const segment of unfueledSegments) {
1043:           const midKm = (segment.startKm + segment.endKm) / 2
1044:           // Use polyline sampling so gap-fill probes are on the actual road.
1045:           const point = routePolyline && routeCumTable
1046:             ? samplePolylineAtKm(midKm, routePolyline, routeCumTable)

1049:                 return interpolatePoint(startLat, startLng, destLat, destLng, fraction)

1052: 
1053:           if (probedMidpoints.has(midpointKey)) continue

1055:           uniqueProbePoints.push({ lat: point.lat, lng: point.lng, key: midpointKey })
1056:         }
1057:

1124:               : station.distanceFromStartKm >= seg.startKm && station.distanceFromStartKm < seg.endKm
1125:             if (!inInterval) continue
1126:             const midKm2 = (seg.startKm + seg.endKm) / 2
1127:             const delta = Math.abs(station.distanceFromStartKm - midKm2)

## Summary of Current Implementation:

### What's Working Well:
1. **Route calculation** - Using Google Directions API to get actual route polyline (not straight-line)
2. **Stop proximity** - Using polyline projection to calculate actual distance from route (not just straight-line)
3. **Segment planning** - Breaking route into day segments based on pace
4. **Fuel station integration** - Fetching fuel stations along the route
5. **Verified vs Alternative stops** - Distinguishing between DB verified stops and Google Places custom stops
6. **Corridor detection** - Auto-detecting route corridor
7. **Remote area handling** - Adaptive boundaries that expand for remote areas

### Gaps & Issues:

1. **No 3-options-per-stop UI** - The current UI shows all stops in a list, not organized by day with 3 options per overnight stop

2. **Stop Priority Logic Issues**:
   - Current: Shows `verifiedStops` and `otherStops` separately
   - Missing: **Recommended marker** on the 1st DB option per segment

3. **Distance Thresholds**:
   - Current: Uses 100km for verified, 25km for Google Places
   - Requirements: 5-10km ideal, 20km acceptable, 30km max (normal), 50km remote
   - Need to update these thresholds

   - Current: Leisure=150km, Moderate=200km, Fast=300km
   - This aligns with requirements!

5. **DB Coverage Gap**:
   - Your DB has limited stops on Melbourne-Sydney Hume corridor
   - Need more stops on this route OR rely on Google Places more

   - Each segment gets multiple stops without a clear "Recommended" indicator
   - UI doesn't display these as 3 choices per overnight point

## Assistant (Plan · Big Pickle · 18.9s)

_Thinking:_

The user is pointing out another issue: The Directions API is only using start and end points to create the route line, but it should also include the stops (waypoints) to create a more accurate route. This is important because the route line should pass through the planned overnight stops to give a more accurate representation of the actual journey.

Let me understand this better:
- Currently: Directions API is called with just origin + destination
- Should be: Directions API should be called with origin + waypoints (the overnight stops) + destination

This way the polyline will actually pass through the stops, making the "distance to route" calculations more accurate.

Let me check how this is currently handled in the codebase and understand the issue better.



Good catch! Let me understand this better. Currently the Directions API creates a route from A→B, but it doesn't pass through your overnight stops. This means:

**Current Flow:**
```
Start (Melbourne) ──────► End (Sydney)
                         ↑
                    Route polyline doesn't pass through
                    stops like Wagga Wagga, Gundagai, etc.
```

```
Start (Melbourne) ──► Stop 1 (Wangaratta) ──► Stop 2 (Gundagai) ──► Stop 3 (Goulburn) ──► End (Sydney)
                    ↑                          ↑                          ↑
              Route includes these waypoints for accurate polyline
```

## Assistant (Plan · Big Pickle · 37.4s)

_Thinking:_

I found the issue. Let me look at lines 1641-1659 in the planner page more closely - this is in the `calculateRoute` function that draws the route on Google Maps.

The comment at line 1643-1645 explicitly says:
```javascript
// Draw the direct A→B corridor only. Stops are rendered as numbered markers
// separately. Including stops as waypoints caused the route to detour to each
// campsite, creating a second visible branch whenever an option was selected.
```

So they intentionally did NOT include waypoints because it caused the route to branch. But the user is saying they DO want the stops to be part of the route polyline - meaning when a user selects a stop as their overnight stop, that stop SHOULD be part of the route.

However, there's a UX problem here - if you include ALL stops as waypoints, the route will show detours to each one. What they probably want is:
- Draw the base route A→B
- When user SELECTS a stop as their overnight stop, add THAT stop as a waypoint so the route goes through it
- Show the "via" route (the actual path) on the map

So there's two separate issues:
1. **Base route drawing** - Currently draws A→B directly without any stops
2. **Selected stops** - Should be included in the route when selected

Looking at the API at `/api/stops/options/route.ts`, it also only uses start/end coordinates:
```javascript
const directionsUrl = buildDirectionsUrl({
  startLat,
  startLng,
  destLat,
  destLng,
  ...
})
```

So both the frontend (planner page) and backend (options API) are not including stops as waypoints.

Let me summarize the full gap analysis now.

---

## Issue: Directions API Not Using Stops as Waypoints

### Current Behavior (Frontend - `planner/[tripId]/page.tsx:1635-1659`)

```javascript
// Draw the direct A→B corridor only. Stops are rendered as numbered markers
// separately. Including stops as waypoints caused the route to detour to each
// campsite, creating a second visible branch whenever an option was selected.
directionsService.route({
  origin: origin,
  destination: destination,
  travelMode: google.maps.TravelMode.DRIVING,
})
```

### Current Behavior (Backend - `/api/stops/options/route.ts:397`)

```javascript
const directionsUrl = buildDirectionsUrl({
  startLat,
  startLng,
  destLat,
  destLng,
  apiKey,
  ...
})
```

Both only use start + destination, NOT the overnight stops.

---

## The Problem

When user selects an overnight stop (e.g., Day 1 stop at km 250), the route line on Google Maps still shows a straight A→B path - it doesn't go through the selected stop. This means:
- The displayed route doesn't reflect where they're actually staying
- Distance calculations may be inaccurate
- User sees a disconnect between their plan and the visual route

---

## What Should Happen

**Scenario: Melbourne → Sydney (900km, 4 days)**

| Day | Overnight Stop | Route Should Include |
|-----|---------------|---------------------|
| Start | Melbourne | - |
| Day 1 | ~250km (near Wangaratta) | Melbourne → Wangaratta |
| Day 2 | ~500km (near Gundagai) | Wangaratta → Gundagai |
| Day 3 | ~750km (near Goulburn) | Goulburn → Sydney |
| Day 4 | Sydney | - |

The route line on map should update to show the ACTUAL path through selected stops, not just a straight line from origin to destination.

---

# Detailed Implementation Plan: Caravan Trip Planner - Stop Selection & Route

## Summary of Issues

| # | Issue | Current State | Desired State |
|---|-------|---------------|---------------|
| 1 | Directions not using stops | Route line A→B only | Route goes through selected overnight stops |
| 2 | Distance thresholds too permissive | 100km (DB) / 25km (Google) | 5-10km ideal → 20km accept → 30km max → 50km remote |
| 3 | No Recommended marker | First DB stop not flagged | First DB stop per segment marked "Recommended" |
| 4 | "3 options per day" UI | Flat list of all stops | Organized by day showing exactly 3 options |
| 5 | Route doesn't update on selection | Map shows A→B line | Map updates to show route through selected stops |

---

## Phase 1: Backend API Changes

### 1.1 Update Distance Thresholds
**File**: `/api/stops/options/route.ts`

**Change**: Line ~519 - Update lateral distance thresholds

```typescript
// CURRENT (lines ~514-523):
const lateralThreshold = isVerified ? 100 : 25

// UPDATED:
const totalRouteKm = routeCumTable[routeCumTable.length - 1]
const progress = totalRouteKm > 0 ? distanceFromStartKm / totalRouteKm : 0

// Remote areas (northbound > 500km into trip get expanded threshold)
const isRemote = northbound && progress > 0.5

const lateralThreshold = isVerified 
  ? (isRemote ? 50 : 30)   // Verified: 30km normal, 50km remote
  : (isRemote ? 30 : 20)   // Google Places: 20km normal, 30km remote
```

### 1.2 Add isRecommended Flag to Segments
**File**: `/api/stops/options/route.ts`

// After determining overnightAnchor (line ~845)

  ...stop,

}))

// Also add to otherStops for consistency

### 1.3 Add Route with Waypoints Support (New Endpoint)

POST /api/route/with-stops

  origin: { lat: number, lng: number },
  destination: { lat: number, lng: number },
  waypoints: Array<{ lat: number, lng: number, name?: string }>
}

## Phase 2: Frontend - Route Drawing

### 2.1 Update Route Calculation to Include Stops

```typescript
// CURRENT (lines 1635-1659):

// UPDATED:
const calculateRoute = useCallback((mapInstance: google.maps.Map) => {
  if (!trip) return
  
  const origin = new google.maps.LatLng(trip.start_lat!, trip.start_lng!)
  const destination = new google.maps.LatLng(trip.destination_lat!, trip.destination_lng!)
  
  // Build waypoints from selected overnight stops (in order)
  const selectedStops = getSelectedStopsInOrder() // Array of {lat, lng, name}
  
  if (selectedStops.length > 0) {
    // Use waypoints for route
    const waypoints = selectedStops.map(stop => ({
      location: new google.maps.LatLng(stop.lat, stop.lng),
      stopover: true
    }))
    
    directionsService.route({
      origin,
      destination,
      waypoints,
      travelMode: google.maps.TravelMode.DRIVING,
      optimizeWaypoints: false, // Keep user-selected order
    }, ...)
  } else {
    // Fallback: direct A→B route
    directionsService.route({
      origin,
      destination,
      travelMode: google.maps.TravelMode.DRIVING,
    }, ...)
  }
}, [trip, selectedSegmentOptionIds, daySegments])
```

### 2.2 Trigger Route Recalculation on Stop Selection
**File**: `src/app/planner/[tripId]/page.tsx` - `handleChooseSegmentOption` (line ~873)

**Change**: Add route recalculation after selection:

    calculateRoute(map)

## Phase 4: Stop Options Panel (Optional Separate Component)

### 4.1 Create DayStopPicker Component
**File**: `src/components/planner/day-stop-picker.tsx` (NEW)

**Purpose**: Replace current flat stop list with organized day-based selection

```typescript
interface DayStopPickerProps {
  segments: RouteSegment[]
  selectedStops: Record<number, string> // segmentIndex -> stopId
  onSelectStop: (segmentIndex: number, stop: RouteStopOption) => void
}

export function DayStopPicker({ segments, selectedStops, onSelectStop }) {
  return (
    <div className="day-stop-picker">
      {segments.map((segment, index) => {
        const options = [...segment.verifiedStops, ...segment.otherStops].slice(0, 3)
        const selected = options.find(o => o.id === selectedStops[index])
        
        return (
          <div key={index} className="day-segment">
            <h3>Day {index + 1}: {segment.startKm} - {segment.endKm} km</h3>
            <div className="options-grid">
              {options.map((option, optIdx) => (
                <button
                  key={option.id}
                  className={`option ${optIdx === 0 ? 'recommended' : ''} ${selected?.id === option.id ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => onSelectStop(index, option)}
                >
                  {optIdx === 0 && (
                    <Badge variant="default">Recommended</Badge>
                  )}
                  <span>{option.location_name}</span>
                  <span>{option.is_verified ? 'DB' : 'Google'}</span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}
```
