lng2: number): number {\n  const R = 6371\n  const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180\n  const dLng = (lng2 - lng1) * Math.PI / 180\n  const a =\n    Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +\n    Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) *\n    Math.sin(dLng / 2) * Math.sin(dLng / 2)\n  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a))\n  return R * c\n}\n\ninterface Waypoint {\n  lat: number\n  lng: number\n  name?: string\n}\n\ninterface GoogleDirectionsRoute {\n  summary?: string\n  overview_polyline?: { points: string }\n  waypoint_order?: number[]\n  legs: Array<{\n    distance: { value: number }\n    duration: { value: number }\n    start_address?: string\n    end_address?: string\n  }>\n}\n\nfunction selectBestDirectionsRoute(routes: GoogleDirectionsRoute[], threshold = 1.1) {\n  if (routes.length <= 1) {\n    return routes[0]\n  }\n\n  const sortedByDistance = routes\n    .filter((route) => route.legs?.[0]?.distance?.value)\n    .slice()\n    .sort((a, b) => a.legs[0].distance.value - b.legs[0].distance.value)\n\n  if (sortedByDistance.length === 0) {\n    return routes[0]\n  }\n\n  const shortestDistance = summary.includes(\"highway\") || summary.includes(\"hwy\") || summary.includes(\"freeway\") || summary.includes(\"motorway\")\n    if (!isHighway) return false\n\n    const ratio = route.legs[0].distance.value / shortestDistance\n    return ratio <= threshold\n  })\n\n  return highwayPreferred || sortedByDistance[0]\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n   }\n\n    const originLat = Number(origin.lat)\n    const originLng = Number(origin.lng)\n    const destLat = Number(destination.lat)\n    const destLng = Number(destination.lng)\n\n    if destination: `${destLat},${destLng}`,\n      mode: \"driving\",\n      key: apiKey,\n    })\n\n    // Add waypoints if provided\n    const sanitizedWaypoints: Waypoint[] = Array.isArray(waypoints)\n      ? waypoints\n          .map((w: { lat?: number; lng?: number; name?: string }) => ({\n            lat: Number(w.lat),\n            lng: Number(w.lng),\n            name: w.name,\n          }))\n          .filter((w) => Number.isFinite(w.lat) && Number.isFinite(w.lng))\n      : []\n\n    if (sanitizedWaypoints.length > 0) {\n      const waypointStr = sanitizedWaypoints\n        .map((w) => (data.status !== \"OK\" || !data.routes?.length) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { \n          success: false, \n          error: data.status || \"No route found\",\n          details: data.error_message \n        },\n        { status: 400 }\n      )\n    }\n\n    const selectedRoute = selectBestDirectionsRoute(data.routes as GoogleDirectionsRoute[])\n    const route = Effects are intended to synchronize state between React and external systems such as manually updating the DOM, state management libraries, or other platform APIs. In general, the body of an effect should do one or both of the following:

## Summary of Changes

### 1. Distance Thresholds (`/api/stops/options/route.ts`)
Updated to respect your spec:
- **Normal**: 30km max (verified) / 20km max (Google Places)
- **Remote**: 50km max (verified) / 30km max (Google Places)

### 2. isRecommended Flag (`/api/stops/options/route.ts`)
Added `isRecommended` and `isDbSource` flags to segment stops:
- First verified DB stop → `isRecommended: true`
- `isDbSource: true` for DB stops, `false` for Google Places

### 3. Route with Waypoints API (`/api/route/with-stops/route.ts`)
New endpoint that generates route polyline passing through selected stops.

### 4. Route Calculation with Waypoints (`planner/[tripId]/page.tsx`)
- Now builds waypoints from selected overnight stops
- Route updates when user selects a stop
- Route recalculates when stop is removed

### 5. Modern Day Stop Picker UI (`planner/[tripId]/page.tsx`)
- Shows exactly 3 options per day (slice(0,3))
- Beautiful card design with:
  - **Recommended badge** (green "★ Recommended") on first option
  - **DB/Google source indicators**: Shield icon + "DB" badge for verified, pin icon + "Google" badge for Google Places
  - Visual distinction: green background for DB, blue for Google
  - Better copy: "Where would you like to stay?" / "Our top pick for this stop"

### 6. Selected Stops on Map
Already working - shows "D1", "D2", etc. markers for selected overnight stops.

### 7. Interactive Fuel Stations
- Click fuel station marker → pans map to that location + zooms to level 14
- Red highlight on focused station

## Analysis of Test Results

### Issues Found

| Problem | Evidence | Location |
|---------|----------|----------|
| **Old API still being used** | Trip generation uses `/api/trips/route.ts` with old 100km threshold | Log shows `[2/3] Fetched stops` using old code |
| **Stops beyond new thresholds** | `distance_to_route_km: 129.1km`, `141.6km`, `102.8km` - all > 30km | Should be rejected with new thresholds |
| **Wrong endpoint for trip creation** | Frontend uses old endpoint for initial stop generation | Our updated `/api/stops/options` not used |

### Current Flow (Wrong)
```
Trip Created → /api/trips/route.ts (old thresholds: 100km/25km) → Shows stops > 30km from route
```

### Should Be
```
Trip Created → /api/stops/options/route.ts (new thresholds: 30km/20km) → Filters out stops > 30km from route
```

## The Problem

The trip generation still runs through the old `/api/trips/route.ts` which has the OLD threshold logic:
- Verified stops: 100km threshold (should be 30km)
- Google Places: 25km threshold (should be 20km)

We updated `/api/stops/options/route.ts` but that's a DIFFERENT endpoint that's called AFTER trip creation (when loading the planner UI).

---

1. **Should I update `/api/trips/route.ts`** to use the new distance thresholds (30km/20km) for the initial trip generation?

2. **Or should I change the frontend** to call `/api/stops/options` directly during trip creation instead of relying on the old endpoint?

3. **The DB has limited coverage** for Melbourne-Sydney (only 4 stops, all >100km from route!). With stricter thresholds, we might get 0 stops. Is that acceptable, or should we keep some fallback?

What's your preference?
